- **scientific-agent-skills-pytorch-lightning**: Deep learning framework (PyTorch Lightning / lightning package). - **scientific-critical-thinking**: Guide for evaluating scientific evidence and claims. Covers study design hierarchy (RCT to expert opinion), effect size interpretation (OR, RR, NNT, Cohen's d), confounding identification, p-value limitations vs clinical significance, GRADE evidence quality assessment, reproducibility, and bias types (selection, information, confounding, reporting). Use when reading a paper, evaluating a preprint, or assessing claims in a literature review. - **scientific-debugging**: Apply the **scientific method to debugging**: generate falsifiable hypotheses, design experiments (tests) to validate them, and systematically narrow down to the root cause. This methodology produces regression tests and documented findings that prevent recurrence. ([Vrooli/Vrooli](https://github.com/Vrooli/Vrooli/tree/HEAD/scenarios/prompt-manager/store/skills/packs/core/scientific-debugging) · ★56)
- **scientific-debugging**: Debug by turning a belief about the code into a falsifiable hypothesis, predicting an observable, and running the one probe that can refute it. Use when a bug resists guesswork and shotgun edits are making the code murkier instead of the cause clearer. ([Amey-Thakur/AI-SKILLS](https://github.com/Amey-Thakur/AI-SKILLS/tree/HEAD/skills/debugging/scientific-debugging) · ★14)
- **scientific-debugging**: 科学调试专家 - 观察(Observe) -&gt; 假设(Hypothesize) -&gt; 实验(Experiment) -&gt; 修复(Fix) ([VidyFoo/antigravity-skill-engine](https://github.com/VidyFoo/antigravity-skill-engine/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/2-audit/scientific-debugging) · ★13)
- **scientific-debugging**: Diagnoses stalled or cross-system failures and wrong, NaN, nondeterministic, or unexpectedly slow scientific results through falsifiable hypotheses across distinct fault classes, with evidence-cited verdicts before any fix. Not for designing benchmarks or pre-registered experiments; use experiment-protocol. ([iowarp/clio-coder](https://github.com/iowarp/clio-coder/tree/HEAD/library/skills/research/scientific-debugging) · ★10 · 2 copies)
- **scientific-debugging**: Hypothesis-driven debugging method — reproduce, isolate, bisect, prove the cause before fixing. Apply whenever observed behavior differs from expected behavior. Independent of project, language, and domain. ([Arlenjim/claude-code-for-non-coders](https://github.com/Arlenjim/claude-code-for-non-coders/tree/HEAD/skills/scientific-debugging) · ★9)
- **scientific-debugging**: Scientific debugging: TRAFFIC method (Zeller), debugging mindset (O'Dell), wolf fence, expert vs novice patterns ([AnjanJ/bodhikit](https://github.com/AnjanJ/bodhikit/tree/HEAD/skills/scientific-debugging) · ★2)
- **scientific-debugging**: Scientific Debugging — hypothesis-driven debugging. Treat bugs not as mysteries but as a chain of falsifiable hypotheses. Observation→Hypothesis→Experiment→Verification loop. Based on Zeller's *Why Programs Fail*. ([ironyjk/software-framework](https://github.com/ironyjk/software-framework/tree/HEAD/scientific-debugging) · ★2)
- **scientific-debugging**: Diagnose reproducible scientific pipeline failures in formats, metadata, design, numerical computation or interpretation; use on unexpected errors or inconsistent results, not to tune analyses until they become significant.
